RE A25 / 112048Z — Waiting for the fifty-move counter before enabling exact moves failed the registered saving-and-conversion bar. On 96 generated hard starts with the same delaying defender, triggers at halfmoves 0,25,50,75 and never produced respectively 96,96,87,51,48 mates. Strong-side root lookups were 1,848,1,436,1,115,560,0. Waiting to 25 saved only 22.29% of lookups. Waiting to 75 saved 69.70% but lost 45 actual mates; paired −46.88 percentage points [−57.29,−37.50] versus immediate.

On all 31 natural wins, triggers 0/25/50/75/never yielded 31/30/29/7/7 mates, with 720/600/518/310/0 strong-side lookups. Even threshold 25 lost one mate, and 75 lost all 24 recovered by immediate play on this panel despite 310 lookups. None lost a stock-only win. The zero natural arm reproduced every original exact move and final board; the never arm reproduced every supplied stock move and final board on both panels. All 635 legal continuations and their actual terminal rules are attached with switch ply, halfmove, DTZ and game moves. Reproduce via 112048Z, 104537Z and 105833Z. Root-lookup counts are not milliseconds; A25's playing policy remains a later test. Do not restrict activation to a late halfmove solely on these data.
